# V17 complete paired original humor coding register

These accepted V17 supporting registers serve the existing Humor/Voice ledger responsibilities as volume evidence appendices. Publication and whole-tranche adjudication remain separate. Original discovery, assignments and authored-time statements remain exact; current errata are explicitly external. Same-model conditional independence and receiving limits are recorded in the [V17 receiving review](../09%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/KONOSUBA_V17_RECEIVING_REVIEW.md). Exact source members are text/part0009.html (C1), part0010.html (C2), part0011.html (C3), part0012.html (F), part0013.html (Ep); excluded bodies/images/holdouts remain unopened in this continuation.

All 179 fixed primary units remain; all 43 soft-scope-touched units stay in the denominator. Both initial categories, full mechanism rationales, uncertainty, nested forms, culture guards and exact routed flag histories are preserved. [Original discovery/operations](KONOSUBA_V17_HUMOR_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.md) is the complete source-first provenance home.

Each table value is compact JSON with one reversible HTML escape for &,<,>,|; JSON carries literal newline/backslash/quote escapes. {"@exact_text":"ID"} expands to the exact string in the named catalog. This is lossless factoring of repeated text, not a shortened rationale. Every unique initial rationale is printed in full, in its own labeled row; its value is factored out of the otherwise exact initial field bundle and restored by the inverse proof. Proof parses actual Markdown rows, reverses escapes/references, and compares every projected field to the immutable initial inputs. Private full novel bases/windows, ruby XML/pairs and long selected quotations are omitted by the declared projection; their hashes/locators and initial analytical metadata remain. No global spacing/reflow is applied to original strings. Native literal joins verify text and location, not by themselves speaker, addressee, literal-gloss accuracy or pragmatic judgment; those remain substantive receiving responsibilities.

This file uses its own HC catalog below. “Current coarse status” is only the conservative pair projection; it is not an overwritten initial code or final root adoption.178 units have two portable initial classes; U17-145 retains P MIXED versus I L1. Original fine labels agree100/179 and differ79/179, including this one mixed/portable disagreement. Those figures are reproducibility descriptions, not translation tests or accuracy.

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L1" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The sinister demon residence is unexpectedly clean, and the narrator grants demons an ordinary dislike of filth. Domestic normalization and the concise concession do the work, without form-specific ambiguity." |

| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "An ominous genre setting receives a mundane cleanliness explanation. Narrator concision and anticlimax matter; the expectation correction is language-general." |

| id | "U17-012" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L0" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "An explicit warning and positive trap detection are rationalized into a helpful shortcut; the chosen press actually traps him and incidentally locates Aqua. Neither incidental benefit nor his ingenuity story changes the causal comic failure." |
| independent_initial_fine_class_exact | "L0" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Explicit sign and detection are rationalized away before pressing the trap; the result exposes his overthinking. The extended internal rhetoric is nested in a clear causal decision reversal." |

| Field | Exact value / declared projection |

| id | "U17-041" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L1" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "An elite-security crisis includes a mundane pet-feeding excuse, while Kazuma infers progress and considers safe waiting. The small official/private priority contrast transfers but is mainly a brief reported verbal aside." |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |

| independent_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |

| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Darkness drops a sword whose loss barely changes her actual offense, earning enemy confidence at little tactical cost. Disarmament is effective deception because skill fit differs from appearance, independent of particular wording." |
| independent_initial_fine_class_exact | "L0" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Disarming the inaccurate swordswoman creates apparent surrender without meaningful loss of offensive value. Capability and information make the situational irony." |

| id | "U17-065" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "A coerced public demand becomes a voyeuristic justification, mixed arousal and group discomfort until moral interruption. | independent_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Public coercion creates incompatible attraction, disgust, rivalry and moral responses. The ordinary monster/human moral ranking is inverted through social framing; particular register enriches it but no Japanese form is indispensable." |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The recent backstabber condemns three-on-one dishonor, then exploits indignation to Bind. The semantic double standard and tactical moral posturing are central, not a language-specific pun." |
| independent_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The dirty fighter demands knightly fairness, exploiting the offended opponent's stance to Bind him. Hypocritical norm invocation is the central pragmatic action." |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L0" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The same rope catches the attached Aqua because Kazuma forgot her tether; he hides the mistake and asks her to wait. The causal self-inflicted ally entanglement, not shortage of rope, drives the new response." |

| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Forgetting the same rope's attached ally creates an unintended extra bound victim. Concealment and a request to wait decorate the concrete causal error; it is not a rope shortage." |

| Field | Exact value / declared projection |

| id | "U17-081" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L1" |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Real roles and cheap stealth are defended as serious caution for humanity, while friends privately interpret habitual cheapness and abandonment risk. Competing moral self-presentation and familiar recipient judgments carry the gag." |

### U17-098

| Field | Exact value / declared projection |
|---|---|
| id | "U17-098" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L1" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The king accepts demanded introduction etiquette only to have his own speech interrupted by real Inferno; Megumin objects to immediate resource use and Yunyun blames friends' request. Timing and role reversal connect the nested replies." |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "A solemn trust demand receives familiarity-based distrust and an ordinary home/labor alternative; painful iron-claw coercion obtains reluctant consent. Relationship knowledge and imposed authority, not polished heroic style, determine the exchange." |

| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "A trust demand receives the answer that familiarity is precisely why trust fails, then pain obtains cooperation. Relational pragmatics and coercive uptake are the primary interaction." |

| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L1" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "A practical abduction/fallback explanation is answered by blunt questions about the corpse rotting or being eaten; Kazuma objects and promises a crystal. The wording's tactlessness and timing matter, while the recovery concern is real." |

| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Practical corpse-recovery questions use spoiling/eating language about the listener, eliciting a perishable-person objection. The coarse lexical frame can be recreated without Japanese wordplay." |

### U17-157

| Field | Exact value / declared projection |
|---|---|
| id | "U17-157" |
| producer_initial_fine_class_exact | "L2" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "After accurately considering a comfortable Japan alternative, Kazuma calls the troublesome world hateful and immediately chooses it. Literal negative appraisal functions as affectionate chosen attachment only through context and irony, not a required Japanese negation." |
